Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in ecology theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 456 and the invariant I(A,B) = 929 mod 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the ecology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^456 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 929-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
